remote_blocked(inst) if REMOTE_KEEP[inst.Name] then return false end if BLOCK_NAME[inst.Name] then return true end return ancestor_blocked(inst) end local function strHasXcode(v) if typeof(v) ~= "string" then return false end if v:find("[Xx]%s*[%-%_%.%:]%s*%d%d?%d?") then return true end if #v >= 24 and v:match("^[A-Za-z0-9%+/_%-]+=?=?$") then return true end if (#v == 32 or #v == 40 or #v == 64) and v:match("^[0-9a-fA-F]+$") then return true end return false end local function scanDeep(v, depth) depth = depth or 0 if depth > 8 then return false end local t = typeof(v) if t == "string" then return strHasXcode(v) elseif t == "table" then local arr, n = true, 0 for k, vd in pairs(v) do if type(k) == "number" and type(vd) == "number" and vd >= 0 and vd <= 255 and vd % 1 == 0 then n = n + 1 else arr = false; break end end if arr and n >= 4 and n <= 256 then local ok, s = pcall(function() local b = {} for i = 1, n do b[i] = string.char(v[i] or 0) end return table.concat(b) end) if ok and strHasXcode(s) then return true end end for k, vd in pairs(v) do if type(k) == "string" and strHasXcode(k) then return true end if scanDeep(vd, depth + 1) then return true end end end return false end local function argsContainXcode(args) for i = 1, #args do if scanDeep(args[i]) then return true end end return false end local function hookMethod(className, methodName, isInvoke) pcall(function()
